Skips upscale by default.\nHandles ComfyUI going down (WSL2 ~90s restart cycle) with retry logic.\n\nUsage:\n  python3 generate-batch-v2.py                     # render all non-rendered panels\n  python3 generate-batch-v2.py --refinements-only  # only the 26 refined panels\n  python3 generate-batch-v2.py --remaining-only    # only never-rendered panels\n  python3 generate-batch-v2.py --all               # everything not \"rendered\" (no refinements)\n  python3 generate-batch-v2.py --test              # test batch (5 representative panels)\n  python3 generate-batch-v2.py --panel 25          # single panel\n  python3 generate-batch-v2.py --dry-run           # show what would be generated\n  python3 generate-batch-v2.py --with-upscale      # enable 4x upscale (slow, needs VRAM)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport urllib.request\nimport urllib.error\nimport uuid\nimport time\nimport os\nimport argparse\nimport sys\n\nCOMFYUI_URL = \"http://192.168.0.121:8188\"\nCHECKPOINT = \"NoobAI-XL-v1.1.safetensors\"\nSTYLE_LORA = \"iLLMythD4rkL1nesV2.safetensors\"\nSTYLE_LORA_WEIGHT = 0.7\nSTYLE_TRIGGER = \"D4rkL1nes\"\nUPSCALE_MODEL = \"4x-UltraSharp.pth\"\nFACE_DETECTOR = \"bbox/face_yolov8m.pt\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = \"/Volumes/synologyNFS/Mardov/panels\"\nJOBS_FILE = \"/Volumes/synologyNFS/Mardov/production/panel-jobs-v2.json\"\n\nWIDTH = 832\nHEIGHT = 1216\nSTEPS = 25\nCFG = 7.0\nSAMPLER = \"euler_ancestral\"\nSCHEDULER = \"karras\"\nSEED_BASE = 44000\n\nNEGATIVE_PROMPT = (\n    \"(text:1.5), (english text:1.5), (writing:1.4), (letters:1.4), (words:1.4), \"\n    \"(caption:1.3), (subtitle:1.3), (title:1.3), (speech bubble:1.3), (logo:1.3), \"\n    \"(watermark:1.4), (signature:1.4), (number:1.2), (symbol:1.2), \"\n    \"(kanji:1.3), (hiragana:1.3), (katakana:1.3), (chinese text:1.3), (alphabet:1.2), \"\n    \"blurry, low quality, deformed, ugly, bad anatomy, disfigured, poorly drawn face, \"\n    \"mutation, extra limbs, extra fingers, missing fingers, bad proportions, cropped, \"\n    \"worst quality, jpeg artifacts, 3d, cgi, realistic photo\"\n)\n\n# Test panels: 1 establishing, 1 character LoRA, 1 action, 1 atmospheric, 1 credits\nTEST_PANELS = [7, 25, 44, 55, 77]\n\n\ndef build_workflow(prompt_text, seed=42, filename_prefix=\"panel\",\n                   use_upscale=False, char_lora_files=None):\n    \"\"\"Build ComfyUI workflow with VAEDecode for VRAM safety.\"\"\"\n    # Prompt already has D4rkL1nes trigger from panel-jobs-v2.json\n    # Add no-text directives\n    if prompt_text.startswith(STYLE_TRIGGER):\n        full_prompt = f\"{prompt_text}, no text, text free\"\n    else:\n        full_prompt = f\"{STYLE_TRIGGER}, {prompt_text}, no text, text free\"\n\n    workflow = {}\n\n    # Node 1: Load checkpoint\n    workflow[\"1\"] = {\n        \"class_type\": \"CheckpointLoaderSimple\",\n        \"inputs\": {\"ckpt_name\": CHECKPOINT}\n    }\n\n    # Node 10: Style LoRA\n    workflow[\"10\"] = {\n        \"class_type\": \"LoraLoader\",\n        \"inputs\": {\n            \"lora_name\": STYLE_LORA,\n            \"strength_model\": STYLE_LORA_WEIGHT,\n            \"strength_clip\": STYLE_LORA_WEIGHT,\n            \"model\": [\"1\", 0],\n            \"clip\": [\"1\", 1]\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Chain character LoRAs\n    last_lora_node = \"10\"\n    if char_lora_files:\n        for idx, lora_file in enumerate(char_lora_files):\n            node_id = str(11 + idx)\n            workflow[node_id] = {\n                \"class_type\": \"LoraLoader\",\n                \"inputs\": {\n                    \"lora_name\": lora_file,\n                    \"strength_model\": 0.55,\n                    \"strength_clip\": 0.55,\n                    \"model\": [last_lora_node, 0],\n                    \"clip\": [last_lora_node, 1]\n                }\n            }\n            last_lora_node = node_id\n\n    # Node 3: Positive prompt\n    workflow[\"3\"] = {\n        \"class_type\": \"CLIPTextEncode\",\n        \"inputs\": {\"text\": full_prompt, \"clip\": [last_lora_node, 1]}\n    }\n\n    # Node 4: Negative prompt\n    workflow[\"4\"] = {\n        \"class_type\": \"CLIPTextEncode\",\n        \"inputs\": {\"text\": NEGATIVE_PROMPT, \"clip\": [last_lora_node, 1]}\n    }\n\n    # Node 5: Empty latent\n    workflow[\"5\"] = {\n        \"class_type\": \"EmptyLatentImage\",\n        \"inputs\": {\"width\": WIDTH, \"height\": HEIGHT, \"batch_size\": 1}\n    }\n\n    # Node 6: KSampler\n    workflow[\"6\"] = {\n        \"class_type\": \"KSampler\",\n        \"inputs\": {\n            \"model\": [last_lora_node, 0],\n            \"positive\": [\"3\", 0],\n            \"negative\": [\"4\", 0],\n            \"latent_image\": [\"5\", 0],\n            \"seed\": seed,\n            \"steps\": STEPS,\n            \"cfg\": CFG,\n            \"sampler_name\": SAMPLER,\n            \"scheduler\": SCHEDULER,\n            \"denoise\": 1.0\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Node 7: VAEDecode\n    workflow[\"7\"] = {\n        \"class_type\": \"VAEDecode\",\n        \"inputs\": {\n            \"samples\": [\"6\", 0],\n            \"vae\": [\"1\", 2],\n        }\n    }\n\n    current_image_node = \"7\"\n\n    # Upscale (optional -- heavy on VRAM)\n    if use_upscale:\n        workflow[\"30\"] = {\n            \"class_type\": \"UpscaleModelLoader\",\n            \"inputs\": {\"model_name\": UPSCALE_MODEL}\n        }\n        workflow[\"31\"] = {\n            \"class_type\": \"ImageUpscaleWithModel\",\n            \"inputs\": {\"upscale_model\": [\"30\", 0], \"image\": [current_image_node, 0]}\n        }\n        workflow[\"32\"] = {\n            \"class_type\": \"ImageScaleBy\",\n            \"inputs\": {\"image\": [\"31\", 0], \"upscale_method\": \"lanczos\", \"scale_by\": 0.5}\n        }\n        current_image_node = \"32\"\n\n    # Node 40: Save image\n    workflow[\"40\"] = {\n        \"class_type\": \"SaveImage\",\n        \"inputs\": {\n            \"images\": [current_image_node, 0],\n            \"filename_prefix\": filename_prefix\n        }\n    }\n\n    return workflow\n\n\ndef queue_prompt(workflow, client_id=None):\n    if client_id is None:\n        client_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n    payload = json.dumps({\"prompt\": workflow, \"client_id\": client_id}).encode()\n    req = urllib.request.Request(\n        f\"{COMFYUI_URL}/prompt\", data=payload,\n        headers={\"Content-Type\": \"application/json\"}\n    )\n    try:\n        resp = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30)\n        result = json.loads(resp.read())\n        return result.get(\"prompt_id\")\n    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:\n        body = e.read().decode()[:500]\n        print(f\"  ERROR: HTTP {e.code} - {body}\")\n        return None\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"  ERROR: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n\ndef wait_for_comfyui(timeout=180):\n    \"\"\"Wait for ComfyUI to become reachable.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Waiting for ComfyUI (up to {timeout}s)...\", end=\"\", flush=True)\n    start = time.time()\n    while time.time() - start < timeout:\n        try:\n            resp = urllib.request.urlopen(f\"{COMFYUI_URL}/system_stats\", timeout=5)\n            json.loads(resp.read())\n            print(\" OK\")\n            return True\n        except:\n            print(\".\", end=\"\", flush=True)\n            time.sleep(5)\n    print(\" TIMEOUT\")\n    return False\n\n\ndef wait_for_completion(prompt_id, timeout=600):\n    start = time.time()\n    empty_queue_count = 0\n    comfyui_down_count = 0\n\n    while time.time() - start < timeout:\n        try:\n            resp = urllib.request.urlopen(f\"{COMFYUI_URL}/history/{prompt_id}\", timeout=10)\n            data = json.loads(resp.read())\n            comfyui_down_count = 0\n\n            if prompt_id in data:\n                outputs = data[prompt_id].get(\"outputs\", {})\n                status = data[prompt_id].get(\"status\", {})\n                if status.get(\"completed\", False) or outputs:\n                    return True, outputs\n                if status.get(\"status_str\") == \"error\":\n                    return False, status.get(\"messages\", [])\n        except urllib.error.URLError:\n            comfyui_down_count += 1\n            if comfyui_down_count >= 6:\n                print(f\"  ComfyUI unreachable for 30s -- WSL2 likely restarted\")\n                return False, \"comfyui_restarted\"\n        except:\n            pass\n\n        elapsed = int(time.time() - start)\n        if elapsed >= 15:\n            try:\n                resp = urllib.request.urlopen(f\"{COMFYUI_URL}/queue\", timeout=10)\n                qdata = json.loads(resp.read())\n                r = len(qdata.get(\"queue_running\", []))\n                p = len(qdata.get(\"queue_pending\", []))\n                if r == 0 and p == 0:\n                    empty_queue_count += 1\n                    if empty_queue_count >= 3:\n                        print(f\"  Queue empty, prompt lost -- assuming restart\")\n                        return False, \"history_lost\"\n                else:\n                    empty_queue_count = 0\n                if elapsed % 30 == 0:\n                    print(f\"  ... {elapsed}s (queue: {r} running, {p} pending)\")\n            except:\n                if elapsed % 30 == 0:\n                    print(f\"  ... {elapsed}s\")\n        time.sleep(5)\n\n    return False, \"timeout\"\n\n\ndef download_output(prompt_id, target_path):\n    try:\n        resp = urllib.request.urlopen(f\"{COMFYUI_URL}/history/{prompt_id}\", timeout=10)\n        data = json.loads(resp.read())\n        if prompt_id in data:\n            outputs = data[prompt_id].get(\"outputs\", {})\n            for node_id, node_output in outputs.items():\n                if \"images\" in node_output:\n                    for img in node_output[\"images\"]:\n                        fname = img[\"filename\"]\n                        subfolder = img.get(\"subfolder\", \"\")\n                        img_url = f\"{COMFYUI_URL}/view?filename={fname}&subfolder={subfolder}&type=output\"\n                        urllib.request.urlretrieve(img_url, target_path)\n                        return True\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"  Download error: {e}\")\n    return False\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Batch panel generator v2 -- status-aware\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--dry-run\", action=\"store_true\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--test\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Generate only 5 test panels\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--panel\", type=int, help=\"Generate only this panel number\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--start\", type=int, default=0)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--with-upscale\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Enable 4x upscale (slow)\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--max-retries\", type=int, default=3)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--refinements-only\", action=\"store_true\",\n                        help=\"Only render panels with status 'needs_refinement'\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--remaining-only\", action=\"store_true\",\n                        help=\"Only render panels with status 'pending'\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--all\", action=\"store_true\",\n                        help=\"Render everything not 'rendered' (pending + needs_refinement)\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    with open(JOBS_FILE) as f:\n        jobs = json.load(f)\n\n    # Status counts from full dataset\n    total = len(jobs)\n    rendered_total = sum(1 for j in jobs if j.get('status') == 'rendered')\n    refinement_total = sum(1 for j in jobs if j.get('status') == 'needs_refinement')\n    pending_total = sum(1 for j in jobs if j.get('status') == 'pending')\n\n    # Filter by panel number first\n    if args.panel:\n        jobs = [j for j in jobs if j[\"panel\"] == args.panel]\n    elif args.test:\n        jobs = [j for j in jobs if j[\"panel\"] in TEST_PANELS]\n\n    # Filter by status\n    if args.refinements_only:\n        jobs = [j for j in jobs if j.get('status') == 'needs_refinement']\n    elif args.remaining_only:\n        jobs = [j for j in jobs if j.get('status') == 'pending']\n    elif args.all:\n        jobs = [j for j in jobs if j.get('status') in ('pending', 'needs_refinement')]\n    else:\n        # Default: render everything that isn't already rendered\n        jobs = [j for j in jobs if j.get('status') != 'rendered']\n\n    jobs = jobs[args.start:]\n\n    # Also skip if output file already exists on disk (double-check)\n    original_count = len(jobs)\n    if not args.refinements_only:\n        filtered = []\n        for j in jobs:\n            fn = j[\"filename\"]\n            draft_fn = fn.replace(\".png\", \"-draft.png\")\n            target = skipped_existing = 0\n\n    print(f\"=== Batch Panel Generator v2 ===\")\n    print(f\"Checkpoint: {CHECKPOINT}\")\n    print(f\"Style LoRA: {STYLE_LORA} ({STYLE_LORA_WEIGHT})\")\n    print(f\"Resolution: {WIDTH}x{HEIGHT} {'+ 4x upscale' if args.with_upscale else '(no upscale)'}\")\n    print(f\"VAE: Standard (lowvram mode handles memory)\")\n    print(f\"Steps: {STEPS}, Sampler: print(f\"  Rendered:         {rendered_total}\")\n    print(f\"  Needs refinement: {refinement_total}\")\n    print(f\"  Pending:          {pending_total}\")\n    print()\n    mode = \"refinements-only\" if args.refinements_only else \\\n           \"remaining-only\" if args.remaining_only else \\\n           \"all non-rendered\" if args.all else \"default (non-rendered)\"\n    print(f\"Mode: {mode}\")\n    print(f\"Jobs to generate: {len(jobs)}\" +\n          (f\" ({skipped_existing} skipped, already on disk)\" if skipped_existing else \"\"))\n    print()\n\n    if print(\"ERROR: ComfyUI did not come online within 120s\")\n            return\n\n        try:\n            resp = urllib.request.urlopen(f\"{COMFYUI_URL}/system_stats\", timeout=10)\n            stats = json.loads(resp.read())\n            dev = stats[\"devices\"][0]\n            vram_free = dev[\"vram_free\"] / 1024**3\n            print(f\"ComfyUI {stats['system']['comfyui_version']} -- {dev['name']}\")\n            print(f\"VRAM free: {vram_free:.1f} GB\")\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"WARNING: Could not get system stats: {e}\")\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    succeeded = failed = 0\n    start_time = time.time()\n\n    for i, job in enumerate(jobs):\n        panel = job[\"panel\"]\n        filename = job[\"filename\"]\n        prompt = target = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, draft_filename)\n\n        # For non-refinement panels, skip if already exists\n        if status != 'needs_refinement' and os.path.exists(target):\n            print(f\"[{i+1}/{len(jobs)}] Panel {panel}: SKIP (exists now)\")\n            continue\n\n        lora_desc = f\" lora={char_loras}\" if char_loras else \"\"\n        status_tag = f\" [{status}]\" if status == 'needs_refinement' else \"\"\n        print(f\"[{i+1}/{len(jobs)}] Panel {panel}: {draft_filename}{lora_desc}{status_tag}\")\n        print(f\"  Prompt: {prompt[:120]}...\")\n\n        # Compute seed -- use offset for refinements to get different output\n        sub_idx = 0\n        marker = f'panel-{panel:02d}'\n        if marker in filename:\n            rest = filename.split(marker)[1]\n            parts = rest.split('-')\n            if len(parts) > 1 and len(parts[1]) > 0:\n                c = parts[1][0]\n                if c.isalpha() and c in 'abcdefgh':\n                    sub_idx = ord(c) - ord('a')\n\n        seed = SEED_BASE + panel * 10 + sub_idx\n        if status == 'needs_refinement':\n            seed += 1  # Offset seed for refined prompts to get fresh output\n        prefix = f\"batch-{panel:02d}\"\n\n        workflow = build_workflow(\n            prompt, seed=seed, filename_prefix=prefix,\n            use_upscale=args.with_upscale,\n            char_lora_files=char_loras if char_loras else None,\n        )\n\n        if args.dry_run:\n            print(f\"  [DRY RUN] seed={seed}\")\n            print()\n            continue\n\n        # Retry loop with ComfyUI recovery\n        for attempt in range(args.max_retries):\n            if attempt > 0:\n                print(f\"  Retry {attempt}/{args.max_retries - 1}...\")\n                if not wait_for_comfyui(timeout=180):\n                    print(f\"  ComfyUI did not recover -- skipping panel {panel}\")\n                    break\n\n            prompt_id = queue_prompt(workflow)\n            if not prompt_id:\n                if attempt < args.max_retries - 1:\n                    time.sleep(10)\n
